## Consecrated Beasts (圣骸兽 / "Sacred Skeleton Beasts")
*Elemental identity: One member per element across five of the seven, with no Cryo and no Geo representative: Consecrated Red Vulture = Pyro, Consecrated Scorpion = Electro, Consecrated Flying Serpent = Anemo, Consecrated Horned Crocodile = Hydro, Consecrated Fanged Beast = Dendro. The element is total — every listed attack on every member deals its own element, with no physical damage in any kit. The element also drives the defensive puzzle: Phagocytic Form raises the beast's OWN element resistance highest (70%), yet the Phagocytic Energy Block is destroyed in one hit only by that same element, so the family deliberately punishes mono-element teams on the damage axis while rewarding carrying that element for the break. All five share 50% Freeze Resistance, which reads as a deliberate anti-Cryo/anti-Freeze clause for a group that has no Cryo member. Statline ratios are identical across all five (hp_ratio 7 / hp_type 2, atk_ratio 6 / atk_type 2) — they are mechanically the same chassis in five elemental skins. Visual palette follows the element: bone-white plating that glows in the element's hue while Phagocytic Form is up and goes dark and inert during the 6-second break window.*

Consecrated Beasts are an elite enemy GROUP (wiki `group = Consecrated Beasts`, `family = Mystical Beasts`, `type = Elite Enemies`), introduced in v3.4 with three members and expanded to five in v3.6. Lore: ordinary wildlife that ate the carcass of a god or comparable greater being and survived the process. They could not fully digest the divine flesh, so it fused outward into bone-like armor plating grafted over their bodies — the Adventurer Handbook line is "A lifeform that became twisted and warped by eating some great being." The devoured consciousness is never fully silenced, and it drives the host toward more violence and carrion. Geographically they are a Sumeru-native group (desert and rainforest), with three known spawns outside it, all sited next to divine remains: a Red Vulture and a Horned Crocodile on Elynas in Fontaine, and a Scorpion on Yashiori Island in Inazuma (Orobashi's corpse). They drop the Consecrated Beast material line (e.g. Marked Shell) used for talent/weapon ascension. The signature fight loop across all five is identical: on aggro the beast immediately enters Phagocytic Form (+60% RES to its own element, +30% all other RES, listed on the stats block as 40% global / 70% own-element). Roughly every 30s it commits to a big telegraphed signature attack, drops out of Phagocytic Form for 6 seconds, and spawns a floating Phagocytic Energy Block. One matching-element hit shatters the block (or six off-element hits); shattering grants the active character a 10-second status that drains 2% max HP per second from the whole party, and the next hit of any kind consumes it to strip 15% of the beast's max HP and paralyze it. Ignore the block or let the timer run out and the beast simply re-armors. So the family's whole design is a repeating "break the armor state, pay a bleed cost, cash in a stun-and-chunk window" cycle. All five also carry a permanent 50% Freeze Resistance.

### Variants
- **Consecrated Red Vulture (Pyro)** — Large hunched bipedal raptor-bird, roughly twice human height, standing forward-leaning on digitigrade clawed legs. Ragged crimson-and-charcoal plumage over an ivory bone carapace that masks the skull and caps the shoulders; the beak reads as part of that bone mask. Broad ragged wings held like arms, doubling as bomb-racks and bludgeons; long counterbalancing tail. Fights with wings flared, silhouette wide and top-heavy. Weapon read: beak, wing-edge, talons, tail. Distinguishing beat for an artist — it leaves the ground, so it needs a flight/hover pose and a slam-landing pose. Has a documented weak point.
  - behavior: Shield state on aggro (Phagocytic Form) and re-armors on a timer. Close range: two melee combos — a 2-hit beak-and-wing (50/70% ATK) and a 4-hit beak-beak-claw-spin (50x2/80/100% ATK), i.e. a small multi-hit and a bigger multi-hit. Mid/long: Slam — flies up and drops on the player for 100% ATK, a single heavy telegraphed hit. Long only: Bombardment — flies in while continuously dropping bombs off its wings, 80% ATK each, a ranged volley. Positional punish: Tail Sweep, 80% ATK, fires only if you are behind it. Signature/shield-break: Phagocytic Barrage — transforms into an orb, charges, then unloads a scattergun of 50% ATK projectiles; this is the move that drops the shield state and spawns the breakable block. Priority is flat (5) across the normal moves with the signature at priority 2 on a 30s cooldown, so the pattern reads as: rotate cheap melee/ranged filler, then commit to the big vulnerable move on a clock.
- **Consecrated Scorpion (Electro)** — Low, wide, ground-hugging arthropod about the size of a cart, belly close to the dirt. Two heavy bone-plated pincers carried forward and high; segmented tail arched over the back ending in an ivory stinger. Violet-black chitin with grafted white bone plates, arcing with Electro while armored. Many legs, so it reads as skittering rather than walking. Weapon read: paired claws plus the overhead stinger — a natural two-weapon silhouette. Has a documented weak point. Notable for a comedic 'Dancing' idle animation on the wiki, which is worth knowing if idle-frame reuse matters.
  - behavior: Shield state on aggro. Close: melee combo 1 is claw-claw-claw (30x2/50% ATK), melee combo 2 is a 6-hit rushdown — both claws, then a flurry of four claw strikes while charging forward, then a stinger projectile (60/30x4/80% ATK). Mid/long (6-35m): Dig — burrows into the ground and strikes from below for 100% ATK, the family's cleanest 'untargetable then emerge' beat. Zone control at close range: Spikes — plugs its tail into the ground and rapidly sprouts spikes all around itself, 80% ATK each, effectively a thorns/AoE-around-self state. Signature/shield-break: Thunderbore Blast — spins and scatters stingers into the air, slams its claws, and the stingers rain down; 30% ATK spin + 70% ATK slam + 100% ATK per falling stinger. Fewest total moves of the five and the shortest signature range band (0-20m), so it plays as the most in-your-face, blender-style member.
- **Consecrated Flying Serpent (Anemo)** — Long sinuous winged serpent that hovers rather than stands — the body coils in the air and never fully rests on the ground. Small bone-armored head with a fanned crest of bone spurs, a pair of membranous forelimb wings set behind the head, and a blade-tipped tail that functions as its ranged launcher. Pale green Anemo glow through the plate seams. This is the family's most mobile and least massive silhouette; it needs hover, coil, teleport-blink and tail-flip poses. Has a documented weak point. The archive text pins its bone shell as compositionally similar to Inazuman Crystal Marrow.
  - behavior: Shield state on aggro. The zoner of the family — the only member with a true ranged rotation and the only one with a pull. Close: a 3-part melee combo (50/70/90% ATK). Long (10-35m): Ranged Combo — waves of 1, then 2, then 3 projectiles, 60% ATK each, a building volley. Cheap filler at any range: Tail Shot, a spinning disc off the tail, 50% ATK, on the shortest cooldown (2-3s) and its own priority tier. Control: two Vacuum Trap variants — it drops a Vacuum Field on the player that continuously drags them to the center for 10% ATK per 0.5s, then either lunges in (melee version, 70% ATK) or teleports onto the field (ranged version, 90% ATK). Signature/shield-break: Scattershot Vortex — conjures a big slow orb that drifts at the player and detonates for 100% ATK. Ten entries in its AI table, the most of any member, and it keeps three moves live even out of Phagocytic Form — the busiest, most kite-resistant kit.
- **Consecrated Horned Crocodile (Hydro)** — Heavy low-slung quadruped reptile, belly near the ground, long armored snout, thick sweeping tail. Single large forward-swept ivory horn off the skull plus a ridge of bone plates running the spine. Massive front-loaded mass; reads as a battering ram with jaws. Deep blue-green scale under bone graft. Needs a submerged/wake pose (it swims through solid ground) and a rolling pose — it literally barrel-rolls sideways at the target. Introduced in v3.6.
  - behavior: Shield state on aggro. The heaviest, most committal melee member. Close: a 4-part combo — horn strike, bite, tail swipe, front flip (60/70/80/100% ATK), an escalating multi-hit that ends on the biggest number. Ranged: fires 3 water blades from horn and tail (60/60/80% ATK). Gap-closer: Subnautical Hunter — submerges into the ground, tracks the player underneath for 10% ATK on contact, then erupts and slams for 80% ATK; the family's second 'burrow, become untargetable, attack from below' beat and the most literal one. Charge: Roll — horn strike then a sideways body-roll into the player (70/90% ATK). Positional punish: Tail Sweep 70% ATK if you get behind it. Uniquely it has a move gated to the BROKEN state — Bite (50% ATK), used only while out of Phagocytic Form, so its damage profile visibly drops during the vulnerable window. Signature/shield-break: Phagocytic Splash — a water eruption under the player followed by an expanding ring wave (80/100% ATK).
- **Consecrated Fanged Beast (Dendro)** — Quadruped saber-toothed feline with panther mass — the Korean localization names it outright as a saber-toothed tiger. Oversized upper canines jutting past the jaw, a bone mask over the face, bone plating across shoulders and spine, tufted blade-tipped tail. Low predatory prowl stance, coiled to spring; the most conventionally 'beast' silhouette of the five and the one that reads as a predator at a glance. Green Dendro light through the plating. Introduced in v3.6; the rarest respawnable elite in the overworld (single spawn at Tunigi Hollow, gated behind The Hymn of Tir Yazad Part 2).
  - behavior: Shield state on aggro. The rushdown brawler. Combo Attack: claw then head strike (60/70% ATK) followed by a leap that lays down 3 trails of vines dealing 80% ATK each — a melee opener that converts into a small zoning field. Lunging Slash: 3 rapid claw strikes then 2 heavy ones (30x3/50/70% ATK), a five-hit flurry. Long-range closer: Savage Roll — balls up and rolls at the player (10% ATK on contact) then sweeps at their location for 80% ATK. Ranged filler: Tail Blade, a Dendro blade launched off the tail, 100% ATK. Signature/shield-break: Phagocytic Ambush — vanishes into mist and reappears BEHIND the player, then raises 2 waves of spiky leaves (80/100% ATK). That vanish-and-reappear-behind beat is unique to this member and is the family's only stealth/reposition read.

**Family structure:** The family is FLAT, not a ladder — and this is the load-bearing structural fact for reskin planning. There is no fodder tier, no leader tier, and no minion tier: all five members are individually-spawning world elites with the same HP/ATK ratios and the same ability template. They are siblings, not a hierarchy. Consequences: (1) The family cannot populate a swarm slot, a summoner slot, or a summoned-add slot — no Consecrated Beast summons anything, buffs an ally, or appears in packs. Every documented encounter is one beast, solo. (2) The only sub-body in the entire family is the Phagocytic Energy Block, a destructible 6-second object, not a combatant — the closest available read on a "shield state" or "orb/pillar you must break", never on an add. (3) The internal differentiation is by MOVEMENT ARCHETYPE rather than power: aerial dive-bomber (Red Vulture), burrowing ambusher (Scorpion, Horned Crocodile), hovering ranged zoner with pull (Flying Serpent), grounded rushdown brawler (Fanged Beast). That is the axis to slice on when assigning reskins. (4) Rarity within the group is a spawn-table artifact, not a tier: the Fanged Beast is the rarest respawnable elite in the overworld (a single spawn at Tunigi Hollow, gated behind The Hymn of Tir Yazad Part 2) but is no stronger than its siblings. If a tier ladder is needed for the mod, it has to be invented on top of the family (e.g. sizing the same body up to boss scale), because the source provides none.

### Raw pairing candidates (pre-curation)
- `Byrdonis (Act 1 elite)` ↔ **Consecrated Red Vulture** (strong) — Cited: tier05/content/act1_pool.yaml lines 104-112 (elite pool, hp [81,84]; Swoop 17 with ramp 2, Peck 3x3, Territorial +1 Str tick). This is the tightest fit in the whole family. Byrdonis is already a large solo bird elite, and the three intents map one-to-one onto Vulture moves an artist can animate without inventing anything: Swoop 17 IS Slam (flies up, drops on the player for a single heavy hit), Peck 3x3 IS Melee Combo 2's rapid beak flurry, and the Territorial strength tick reads as the Archive's stated character — the devoured consciousness driving it toward escalating violence, animated as a wing-flare screech. Elite tier on both sides, single body on both sides, no summons on either side. The ramp-on-Swoop escalation even matches the source's flat-priority rotation punctuated by a heavier committed move. Nothing in the statline needs to move.
- `Owl Magistrate (Act 3 normal, 234 HP / A8 243)` ↔ **Consecrated Red Vulture** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 2.1 Act 3 normals table (Magistrate Scrutiny 16; Peck Assault 4x6; Judicial Flight (Soar); Verdict 33 + Vulnerable). Offered as the higher-scale alternate to Byrdonis: the Vulture's kit reads even more exactly here. Peck Assault 4x6 is the beak flurry at full length, Judicial Flight/Soar is the airborne phase before Slam, Verdict 33 is the landing, and Magistrate Scrutiny 16 is a mid-size committed hit. At 234 HP it also matches the chunky elite feel a Consecrated Beast carries in the source, where Byrdonis at ~82 HP undersells it. The cost of the swap is pure flavor, not mechanics: the 'magistrate passing judgement' read is lost and replaced with a feral scavenger, and 'Verdict' has to be renamed. Since a reskin swaps names as well as art, that is a taste call rather than a mechanical one — hence still strong, with the caveat flagged for the ruling.
- `Tunneler (Act 2 normal, 87 HP)` ↔ **Consecrated Horned Crocodile** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 1.1 Act 2 Hive normals (Bite 13; Burrow +32 Block untargetable; Attack from Below 23; Emerging Strike stun). Subnautical Hunter is the same move set already animated in Genshin: the Crocodile submerges INTO SOLID GROUND, becomes effectively unhittable while it tracks you underneath for chip damage, then erupts and slams. Burrow's +32 Block/untargetable is the submerge, Attack from Below 23 is the eruption, Emerging Strike's stun is the slam's knockdown, and Bite 13 is its literal Bite move. The whole intent sequence survives untouched, and the burrow-then-emerge silhouette is exactly what an artist needs two poses for anyway. The only stretch is scale: 87 HP is an Act 2 normal, while a Consecrated Beast is a world elite, so the body may read as more imposing than the statline earns.
- `The Insatiable (Act 2 boss, 321 HP / A8 341)` ↔ **Consecrated Horned Crocodile** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 1.3 Act 2 bosses (Liquify Ground: 4 Sandpit + 6 Frantic Escape cards shuffled in; then cycle Thrash 8x2, Lunging Bite 28, Salivate +2 Str). The thematic fit is unusually exact — the entire lore premise of a Consecrated Beast is a creature warped by DEVOURING a greater being, and The Insatiable's identity is eating you alive. Lunging Bite 28 is the Crocodile's 4-part combo ending on its bite/flip; Thrash 8x2 is the tail-and-horn sweep; Salivate's +2 Str reads perfectly as a beast working its jaws. Liquify Ground's Sandpit is the harder call but lands well on this body: the Crocodile already SUBMERGES INTO GROUND and its signature Phagocytic Splash erupts a pool under the player and rings outward — a sandpit/quicksand field is the same visual grammar, and Sumeru desert is where this beast actually spawns. Boss scale suits the Consecrated frame better than any normal does. Frantic Escape's 'you get eaten in X turns' timer needs a bespoke card art, but the mechanic is untouched.
- `Mawler (Act 1 hard pool, 72 HP)` ↔ **Consecrated Fanged Beast** (strong) — Cited: tier05/content/act1_pool.yaml lines 62-71 (Claw 4x2, Rip and Tear 14, Roar apply 3 Vulnerable). The Fanged Beast is a saber-toothed big cat and Mawler's three intents are the three things a big cat does. Claw 4x2 is Lunging Slash's rapid claw strikes, Rip and Tear 14 is its heavy claw finisher, and Roar's 3 Vulnerable is the snarl-and-flatten beat every predator silhouette sells for free. No summons on either side, single body on both, and the Beast's low prowl stance reads as a hard-pool bruiser without any pose invention. The one thing the ruling should weigh: at 72 HP this is a normal, and the Fanged Beast is a rare world elite, so the reskin cashes in the family's most prestigious body on a mid-tier slot. Mechanically nothing changes.
- `Soul Nexus (Act 3 elite, 234 HP / A8 254)` ↔ **Consecrated Flying Serpent** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 2.2 Act 3 elites (opens Soul Burn 29, then random no-repeat Soul Burn 29 / Maelstrom 6x4 / Drain Life 18 + 2 Vuln + 2 Weak; described as a pure damage check). Soul Nexus is a gimmick-free stat block that needs a body, and the Flying Serpent is the family's ranged member with the busiest kit — perfect donor. Maelstrom 6x4 is the Ranged Combo's building 1-2-3 projectile waves or the Vacuum Field itself (a literal vortex that pulls and ticks). Soul Burn 29 is Scattershot Vortex, the slow conjured orb that drifts in and detonates. Drain Life's debuff bundle reads as the Vacuum Trap dragging you in and leaving you battered. Elite tier matches elite family, and the Serpent's hovering, teleporting profile sells 'nexus' far better than a grounded quadruped would. The randomised no-repeat move selection also matches the source's flat-priority random-among-ties AI.
- `Ceremonial Beast (Act 1 Overgrowth boss, 252 HP)` ↔ **Consecrated Fanged Beast** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 4 Act 1 boss pools, Overgrowth table (P1 until HP<=150: Stamp then Plow 18 + 2 Str every turn; P2 3-cycle: Beast Cry applying 1 Ringing, Stomp 15, Crush 17 + 3 Str; threshold gimmick: at <=150 HP it stuns and loses ALL Strength). The gimmick is the reason this pairing is worth a ruling — 'hit a threshold, the beast is stunned and loses its accumulated state' is a near-transcription of the Phagocytic Form loop, where breaking the Energy Block paralyzes the beast and strips 15% of its max HP. A player reading the Consecrated Beast art will already expect a break-the-armor moment, so the threshold stun lands as intentional design rather than an arbitrary boss rule. Beast Cry's Ringing is the roar, and the Stamp/Stomp/Crush vocabulary is quadruped-native. Boss scale is also the honest tier for this family. Note for the ruling: the base beast is described as a heavy stamper, which arguably suits the Horned Crocodile's charge/roll body better than the cat's — both are live options.
- `Spiny Toad (Act 2 normal, 116-119 HP)` ↔ **Consecrated Scorpion** (plausible) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 1.1 (Protruding Spikes +5 Thorns; Spike Explosion 23 losing 5 Thorns; Tongue Lash 17). The Scorpion has a move that is exactly this: Spikes, where it plugs its tail into the ground and rapidly sprouts a ring of spikes around itself for 80% ATK each. Protruding Spikes accumulating Thorns is the tail planting and the field growing; Spike Explosion 23 spending those Thorns is Thunderbore Blast's stinger-rain payoff, which likewise dumps the accumulated threat all at once. Tongue Lash 17 becomes the stinger projectile that ends the Scorpion's second melee combo. Both bodies are low, wide, ground-hugging and squat, so the silhouette swap is quiet. Marked plausible rather than strong because the Thorns/reactive-damage read is more passive-defensive than the Scorpion's aggressive claw-rushdown personality, and the base has no burrow beat to use the Scorpion's Dig.
- `Hunter Killer (Act 2 normal, 121 HP)` ↔ **Consecrated Scorpion** (plausible) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 1.1 (Tenderizing Goop applying Tender; Bite 17; Puncture 7x3). Puncture 7x3 is a natural stinger read — three quick jabs from the arched tail — and Tenderizing Goop applying a damage-amplifying debuff is exactly what an envenoming stinger should do, which is a rare case where a base mechanic gets MORE legible on the new body rather than less. Bite 17 maps onto the paired-claw strike that opens the Scorpion's melee combo 1. The Hive setting is already arthropod-coded, so a scorpion sits in the zone without friction. Plausible rather than strong because the base has no equivalent to the Scorpion's two loudest beats (Dig and the ground-spike ring), so a chunk of the donor animation set goes unused, and 'Hunter Killer' implies a lean stalker where the Scorpion is broad and armored.
- `Frog Knight (Act 3 normal, 191 HP / A8 199)` ↔ **Consecrated Fanged Beast** (plausible) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 2.1 (Tongue Lash 13 + Frail; Strike Down Evil 21; For the Queen +5 Str; Beetle Charge 35; Plating 15). The reason to look at this one is Plating 15 — a flat damage-cap armor state is the closest thing the base roster has to Phagocytic Form, and it lets the Consecrated art explain a mechanic that is otherwise just a number. Beetle Charge 35 is Savage Roll, the Fanged Beast literally balling up and rolling at the player from range. Tongue Lash becomes Tail Blade, the ranged projectile off the tail. Plausible not strong: 'Frog Knight' and 'For the Queen' carry a chivalric, serving-a-monarch identity that a feral saber-tooth throws away entirely, and Strike Down Evil implies a wielded weapon the beast does not have. The mechanics survive cleanly; the fiction does not.
- `Lagavulin Matriarch (Act 1 boss, 222 HP)` ↔ **Consecrated Fanged Beast** (plausible) — Cited twice: tier05/content/act1_pool.yaml lines 170-189 (as modeled: sleep_turns 3, Slash 19, Disembowel 9x2, Slash 12, block 12 beat, +2 Str Soul Siphon half) and docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 4 Underdocks table (full statline including Plating 12 while asleep and the permanent player -2 Str/-2 Dex drain). The armored-dormant-then-maul shape suits a bone-plated apex predator, and Slash/Disembowel are pure claw vocabulary the saber-tooth already animates via Lunging Slash. Soul Siphon's stat theft is thematically superb on this family — a beast that grows stronger by consuming what it feeds on is the Consecrated Beast origin story stated outright. Held at plausible for one reason the ruling should weigh: Consecrated Beasts enter Phagocytic Form IMMEDIATELY on aggro and are never dormant, so the 3-turn sleep opener is off-model for the family and would need either an invented curled-sleeping pose or acceptance that the beast starts armored-and-waiting rather than asleep.
- `Globe Head (Act 3 normal, 148 HP / A8 158)` ↔ **Consecrated Scorpion** (plausible) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 2.1 (Shocking Slap 13 + Frail; Thunder Strike 6x3; Galvanic Burst 16 + Str; Galvanic 6). The elemental identity is a free exact match — the base enemy is already electric (Shocking, Thunder, Galvanic) and the Scorpion is the family's Electro member, so every VFX pass carries over without recoloring. Thunder Strike 6x3 maps to the stinger jabs, Galvanic Burst 16 with a Strength rider maps to the tail slam that opens Thunderbore Blast, Shocking Slap is the claw. Plausible rather than strong on silhouette alone: 'Globe Head' reads as a floating spherical head, which is the opposite of a wide multi-legged arthropod, so this reskin changes the encounter's visual footprint substantially even though the intents and the element line up perfectly. Worth ruling on precisely because the mechanical case and the silhouette case disagree.
- `Mecha Knight (Act 3 elite, 300 HP / A8 320)` ↔ **Consecrated Red Vulture** (stretch) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 2.2 (Charge 25, then cycle Flamethrower putting 4 Burns into hand, Windup +15 Block +5 Str, Heavy Cleave 35; Artifact 3). Two hooks make this interesting: Artifact 3 is a stacking debuff-negation state that a Consecrated Beast's Phagocytic Form explains better than a mech chassis does, and the Flamethrower's Burn generation matches the Vulture's Pyro identity exactly. Heavy Cleave 35 is the Slam landing; Charge 25 is the opening dive. Held at plausible-leaning-stretch: the base is a bipedal armored humanoid mech with a wielded cleaver, and while the Vulture is also bipedal and armored, it fights with beak, wing and talon — the Windup/Cleave animation grammar of a swung weapon does not exist on this body and would read as a compromise. Offered mainly because the Pyro/Burn and Artifact/Phagocytic alignments are too clean to leave unlisted.
- `Slimed Berserker (Act 3 normal, 266 HP / A8 276)` ↔ **Consecrated Horned Crocodile** (stretch) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md section 2.1 (Vomit Ichor shuffling 10 Slimed cards; Furious Pummeling 4x4; Leeching Hug applying Weak and gaining Str; Smother 30). The mass is right — at 266 HP this is the biggest normal in the base roster, and the Horned Crocodile is the family's heaviest body, so the sheer bulk reads correctly. Furious Pummeling 4x4 is the 4-part horn-bite-tail-flip combo, Smother 30 is the erupting slam, and Leeching Hug's drain-and-grow fits the devouring-beast lore. Listed as a stretch because Vomit Ichor is the fight's defining mechanic and it is deck pollution — no Consecrated Beast pollutes, disgorges, or interacts with the player's deck at all, and a crocodile spraying ten Slimed cards is inventing a behavior the family does not have. If the ruling wants this HP tier reskinned, The Insatiable pairing above is the cleaner route to the same body.

**Sources:** https://genshin-impact.fandom.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvslots=main&rvprop=content&format=json&titles=Consecrated_Beast (family page: lore, Phagocytic loop, type list, 3.4 change history, non-Sumeru spawn trivia); https://genshin-impact.fandom.com/api.php?...&titles=Consecrated_Red_Vulture (Pyro; stats hp_ratio 7 / atk_ratio 6; full ability + AI priority/cooldown table); https://genshin-impact.fandom.com/api.php?...&titles=Consecrated_Scorpion (Electro; abilities incl. Dig, Spikes, Thunderbore Blast; AI table); https://genshin-impact.fandom.com/api.php?...&titles=Consecrated_Flying_Serpent (Anemo; Vacuum Trap variants, Scattershot Vortex; 10-row AI table; Crystal Marrow archive note); https://genshin-impact.fandom.com/api.php?...&titles=Consecrated_Horned_Crocodile (Hydro; Subnautical Hunter, Roll, broken-state-only Bite; 3.6 change history); https://genshin-impact.fandom.com/api.php?...&titles=Consecrated_Fanged_Beast (Dendro; Phagocytic Ambush, Savage Roll, Tail Blade; Tunigi Hollow rarity trivia; 3.6); https://genshin-impact.fandom.com/api.php?action=query&list=categorymembers&cmtitle=Category:Consecrated_Beasts (confirms exactly five members as of the current wiki state — no Natlan-era additions); https://genshin-impact.fandom.com/wiki/Consecrated_Beast; https://game8.co/games/Genshin-Impact/archives/402235 (Consecrated Beast enemy guide); https://game8.co/games/Genshin-Impact/archives/409493 (Consecrated Fanged Beast guide); BASE ROSTER — docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md (sections 1.1 Act 2 normals, 1.2 Act 2 elites, 1.3 Act 2 bosses, 2.1 Act 3 normals, 2.2 Act 3 elites, 2.3 Act 3 bosses, 3 Underdocks, 4 Act 1 boss pools); BASE ROSTER — tier05/content/act1_pool.yaml (Act 1 as modeled: easy/hard/elite/boss pools; mawler L62-71, byrdonis L104-112, bygone_effigy L114-129, phantasmal_gardener L131-148, vantom L156-168, lagavulin_matriarch L170-189); BASE ROSTER — tier0/content/encounters/battery.yaml and punisher.yaml (frozen abstract calibration battery — swarmling/grinder/sleeper/tank_boss/punisher; deliberately NOT paired, these are unthemed calibration fixtures frozen 2026-07-19, not roster content)

---

## Natlan tribal & saurian enemies (Sauroform Tribal Warriors · Natlan Saurians · Wayob Manifestations)
*Elemental identity: Six-element family with strict tribe→element→saurian binding, layered on a Physical baseline.

- Geo — Children of Echoes / Nanatzcayan / Forged Sand warriors / Tepetlisaurus. Drills, burrowing, crystal shards, quakes. Ochre-sandstone palette.
- Dendro — Scions of the Canopy / Huitztlan / Swiftstep warriors / Yumkasaurus. Grapple-tongues, boomerang blades, hook-swings, leaf VFX. Green-tan palette.
- Hydro — People of the Springs / Meztli / Flowing Blade warriors / Koholasaurus. Wave-dashes, harpoons, water cannons, surf-riding. Teal palette.
- Pyro — Flower-Feather Clan / Tlalocan / Winged Warriors / Qucusaurus. Airborne axekicks, fireball slings, explosive hammers, feather barrages. Red-orange plumage palette.
- Cryo — Masters of the Night-Wind / Mictlan / Spiritspeaker warriors / Iktomisaurus. Weapon infusion, icicle rain, frost fields, spirit-mask motifs. Black-violet-pale-blue palette.
- Electro — Collective of Plenty / Teteocan / Ferocious Warriors / Tatankasaurus. Ram charges, triple smashes, slingshot fan-volleys. Purple-and-shaggy-hide palette.

Structural quirks worth carrying into a reskin: (a) tribal guardians deal Physical on their basic combo and only elemental on their one signature move — so an "attack / attack / big elemental attack" rotation is native to the body; (b) Wayob Manifestations are damage-immune while warded, i.e. a shield/untargetable state is canon; (c) Iktomisaurus/Koholasaurus warriors have a persistent, non-expiring "Enhanced State" that raises ATK 20% — a native Strength-ramp read.*

Natlan's native combat roster splits into three interlocking groups that share one visual language: volcanic-Mesoamerican/Polynesian/Afro-Caribbean tribal dress, obsidian-and-bone ornament, saurian anatomy, and per-tribe elemental coding.

(1) SAUROFORM TRIBAL WARRIORS ("Other Human Factions" in wiki taxonomy) are the human warriors of Natlan's six great tribes — Children of Echoes/Nanatzcayan (Geo), Scions of the Canopy/Huitztlan (Dendro), People of the Springs/Meztli (Hydro), Flower-Feather Clan/Tlalocan (Pyro), Masters of the Night-Wind/Mictlan (Cryo), Collective of Plenty/Teteocan (Electro). Per the Adventurer Handbook they have "gradually acquired the traits" of the Saurians they live and fight alongside, and apply them in battle. They are hostile to the Traveler as trial-opponents/guards; 200+ spawn across Natlan, alone and in warbands. Every one deals Physical DMG plus one tribe-coded elemental special.

(2) NATLAN SAURIANS ("Mystical Beasts") are the six draconic species themselves — legend says ancient dragons lost their power and adapted into environment-bound forms. Adults can be *indwelled* (ridden/piloted) by the player, which is why the wild ones read as neutral-to-territorial rather than malevolent. Whelps/chicks are the fodder tier.

(3) WAYOB MANIFESTATIONS ("Mystical Beasts", elite) are the ancestral great-spirits the tribes worship, incarnated as floating obsidian totems. They are the family's elite spine: each deploys a domed "Arena: Moment of Trial" that drains all party Energy while granting a huge shield, and converts to an elemental shield if you fail to break it in time.

Where they appear: all across Natlan (5.0+) — Ochkanatlan, Tezcatepetonco Range, Toyac Springs, Sulfurous Veins, Atocpan, the Stadium of the Sacred Flame region — plus Spiral Abyss, Imaginarium Theater, and Stygian Onslaught rotations. Named overworld "Local Legend" versions of many variants carry extra phases (parries, shields, arena hazards).

### Variants
- **Forged Sand Interrogator (Geo · Children of Echoes)** — Average-build woman, ochre and sandstone wraps, rock-plate shoulder guard, carved drill-horn half-mask. One-handed blade held low, weight forward.
  - behavior: Melee rush. Swing (2-hit combo) · Stab (single lunge) · Headspin — a spinning headfirst drill-charge that closes distance and lands as Geo on contact.
- **Forged Sand Shieldbreaker (Geo)** — Heavier man, same ochre kit with a broad chest plate; two-handed hafted weapon, wide braced stance.
  - behavior: Melee rush, heavy. Swing (2 equal hard hits) · Smash (butt-stab then overhead slam) · Headspin drill-charge (Geo).
- **Forged Sand Javelineer (Geo)** — Unarmoured brawler, bandolier of two stone-tipped javelins and a knife belt; bare-fisted guard.
  - behavior: Melee + limited ranged volley. Punch (2-hit) · Kick · Javelin Throw (2 charges only, then permanently downgrades to weak Knife Throw) · Headspin drill-charge (Geo).
- **Swiftstep Seeker (Dendro · Scions of the Canopy)** — Lean woman, green-tan scouting leathers, vine and leaf accents, long tail-sash. One-handed sword, light on her feet.
  - behavior: Melee rush + returning ranged. Slash (2-hit) · Stab (lunge) · Boomerang — throws the sword out and back in an arc, Dendro along the path; ricochets off terrain and staggers her.
- **Swiftstep Storm Scout (Dendro)** — Male counterpart to the Seeker; same canopy-scout kit, sword.
  - behavior: Identical rotation to Seeker: Slash 2-hit · Stab · Boomerang blade-throw (Dendro).
- **Swiftstep Armed Courier (Dendro)** — Bulkier canopy scout with a courier's pack; hefts a claymore across the shoulder.
  - behavior: Melee heavy + returning ranged. Slash (2 hard hits) · Cleave (butt-stab into downward cleave) · Boomerang claymore-throw (Dendro), which knocks him back if it hits terrain.
- **Flowing Blade Skirmisher (Hydro · People of the Springs)** — Teal wetsuit-like wraps, shell and fin ornaments, water-worn beads. Unarmed guard, harpoon rig on the back.
  - behavior: Melee rush. Punch (2-hit) · Kick · Triple Wavecrash — three consecutive dash-charges through the player, Hydro on contact.
- **Flowing Blade Grease-Saw (Hydro)** — Same reef palette, heavier; carries a broad saw-edged blade like a surf-fin. Braced low stance.
  - behavior: Melee rush. Swing (2-hit) · Stab · Triple Wavecrash triple dash-charge (Hydro).
- **Flowing Blade Harpoon-Thrower (Hydro)** — Fisher-warrior with two barbed harpoons slung at the hip and a knife belt.
  - behavior: Melee + ranged volley. Punch (2-hit) · Kick · Harpoon Throw (2 charges, then downgrades to Knife Throw) · Triple Wavecrash (Hydro).
- **Winged Warrior: Skysoarer (Pyro · Flower-Feather Clan)** — Red-orange feathered cloak and wing-shoulder plumes, beaked mask. Unarmed, high springy guard.
  - behavior: Melee rush with an aerial finisher. Punch (2-hit) · Kick · Flying Axekick — airborne flip into a heel-cleave, the family's single biggest guardian hit (Pyro).
- **Winged Warrior: Feathershedder (Pyro)** — Plumed woman with a one-handed sword and trailing feather-mantle.
  - behavior: Melee rush. Slash (2-hit) · Stab (lunge) · Flying Axekick aerial heel-cleave (Pyro).
- **Winged Warrior: Skymaster (Pyro)** — Largest of the feathered trio; claymore, heavy plumed pauldrons, ceremonial headdress.
  - behavior: Melee heavy. Slash (2 hard hits) · Cleave (butt-stab into downward cleave) · Flying Axekick aerial heel-cleave (Pyro).
- **Spiritspeaker Warrior: Determinator (Cryo · Masters of the Night-Wind)** — Black-violet robes over wraps, pale spirit mask with glowing eye-slits, trailing wind-ribbons. One-handed sword.
  - behavior: Melee rush with a visible buff-cast. Slash (2-hit) · Stab · Infusion — casts a spell to imbue the blade with Cryo, then delivers an escalating 3-slash chain (70/85/100%).
- **Spiritspeaker Warrior: Psychokinetic (Cryo)** — Male medium in the same Mictlan mask-and-ribbon kit; sword held in a loose telekinetic-looking guard.
  - behavior: Melee rush + buff-cast. Slash (2-hit) · Stab · Infusion cast into a 3-slash Cryo chain.
- **Spiritspeaker Warrior: Contemplator (Cryo)** — Heavy Mictlan ritualist, claymore, layered death-mask and shroud.
  - behavior: Melee heavy + buff-cast. Slash (2 hard hits) · Cleave · Infusion cast into a 3-slash Cryo chain.
- **Ferocious Warrior: Human Ram (Electro · Collective of Plenty)** — Bulkiest guardian frame, shaggy bison pelt over the shoulders, curved horn headpiece, purple accents. Hafted weapon.
  - behavior: Melee rush. Swing (2-hit) · Stab · Ram Crash — shoulder-first straight-line charge, Electro on contact.
- **Ferocious Warrior: Wrestler (Electro)** — Same bison kit, thicker; heavy two-handed weapon, low wrestler's crouch.
  - behavior: Melee heavy. Swing (2 hard hits) · Smash (butt-stab into overhead) · Ram Crash charge (Electro).
- **Ferocious Warrior: Grappler (Electro)** — Bare-fisted bruiser with a horned pauldron and two javelins strapped to the back.
  - behavior: Melee + ranged. Punch (2-hit) · Kick · Javelin Throw (2 charges, then Knife Throw) · Ram Crash charge (Electro).
- **Tepetlisaurus Warrior: Shard Striker (Geo · Saurian-Crowned)** — Large man in a full Tepetlisaurus skull-crown with drill-horn crest and stone pauldrons; two-handed war hammer.
  - behavior: Elite melee. Melee Combo (up to 3 hammer swings, may cut short) · Earthshatter Hammer (overhead Geo slam) · Shard Striker — summons a massive crystal and shatters it, launching Geo shards forward.
- **Tepetlisaurus Warrior: Rockbreaker Blade (Geo)** — Crowned champion wielding a powered chain-saw blade; stone plate armor, revving VFX.
  - behavior: Elite melee with a shield state. Melee Combo (3 saw swings + a 3-tick rev) · Earthshake Saw (planted saw sends a Geo shockwave) · Rockbreaker Blade (crystal shatter). Local Legend: gains a total-immunity shield at 70% HP breakable only by perfect-dodge counters, and swaps in a 5-hit Saw Dance.
- **Yumkasaurus Warrior: Whirling Leaves (Dendro)** — Canopy champion in a Yumkasaurus crown with a long tongue-like grapple cord; powered saw blade.
  - behavior: Elite melee + gap-close. Two melee saw combos · Hook and Slash (grapples himself into the player riding the saw, then a horizontal slash) · Whirling Leaves (2 infused lashes, then hurls and retracts the blade).
- **Yumkasaurus Warrior: Flowing Skyfire (Dendro)** — Woman with a wide-mouth cannon on a grapple thread, leaf-plated crown; fights at range, swinging on lines.
  - behavior: Ranged volley + AoE rain. Melee Combo (2 cannon strikes) · Scattershot (hooks around, fires 8 bullets at once) · Cannon Shot · Flowing Skyfire — swings the cannon on its thread then scatters 9 cannonballs into the sky that rain down. Local Legend adds 3 destructible aerial grapple points she snipes from.
- **Koholasaurus Warrior: Waveshuttler (Hydro)** — Woman riding/mounting a huge slingshot like a surfboard; whale-lizard crown, finned shoulders.
  - behavior: Ranged volley + surf-charge. Melee Combo (2 slingshot strikes) · Scattershot (6 small projectiles) · Waterball Sling (up to 9 projectiles in succession; ends early if you close) · Waveshuttler (surfs the slingshot into a charge). Local Legend adds a Cryo spray-can zone hazard and a Frozen-punish leap.
- **Koholasaurus Warrior: Reefsplitter (Hydro)** — Woman with a heavy water cannon used as both club and gun; wave-dashes constantly, reef-plate armor.
  - behavior: Mobile ranged. Wavedash (sidestep before/after attacks) · Melee Combo (2 cannon strikes) · Rapid Fire (12 water bullets) · Cannon Shot (3 large water balls) · Reefsplitter (surfs the cannon into a charge).
- **Qucusaurus Warrior: Heartstar Hammer (Pyro)** — Broad man in a Qucusaurus feather-crown, molten-glowing war hammer, ember VFX trailing.
  - behavior: Elite melee, explosive. Melee Combo (3 hammer swings) · Explosive Strike (overhead smash + delayed Pyro explosion, blast-jumps him back) · Heartstar Hammer (leaping strike). Local Legend spawns destructible Molten Barbs and detonates them for a huge AoE.
- **Qucusaurus Warrior: Blazing Sky (Pyro)** — Woman with a mounted slingshot and a flame-plumed crown; fires from a braced kneel.
  - behavior: Ranged volley. Melee Combo (2 slingshot strikes) · Ignition Burst (one fireball that SPLITS into 3 smaller ones) · Fireball Sling (up to 6 in succession, ends early if you close) · Blazing Sky (one large fireball).
- **Iktomisaurus Warrior: Icy Tidebearer (Cryo)** — Masked Mictlan champion in a frost-crested saurian crown; powered saw rimed with ice.
  - behavior: Elite melee with a permanent ramp. Melee Combo (3 saw swings + rev) · Enhanced State — a Cryo burst around himself that permanently raises his ATK 20% and unlocks stronger moves; the combo converts to Cryo and lengthens · Sawblade (thrown spinning disc, enhanced only).
- **Iktomisaurus Warrior: Cryocrystal Cannon (Cryo)** — Woman with a large ice cannon; blinks and drifts across the ground, frost crown and ribbons.
  - behavior: Ranged artillery with a ramp. Melee Combo (2 cannon strikes) · Cannon Shot (2 balls, unenhanced) · Enhanced State (permanent +20% ATK) · Enhanced Cannon Shot (3 balls) · Airstrike (mortar shell onto your position). Local Legend adds Parry (blocks 3 attacks then blinks), explosive-barrel traps, homing Icicle Rain, and a Fusion Nova arena finale.
- **Tatankasaurus Warrior: Skybreaker (Electro)** — Heaviest crowned frame — bison-horn crown, shaggy mantle, enormous two-handed hammer. Reads as an armored knight.
  - behavior: Elite melee, charging. Melee Combo (3 hammer swings; dashes in from range) · Triple Smash (3 escalating Electro hammer impacts) · Skybreaker (charges the player and swings twice for the family's biggest paired hit).
- **Tatankasaurus Warrior: Spiritlight Chaser (Electro)** — Woman with a mounted slingshot and horned crown; darts in and out, arcs of Electro trailing.
  - behavior: Hit-and-run melee + fan volley. Melee Combo (3 slingshot strikes) · Rushing Strike (dash into 2 strikes, kick first if you're far) · Spiritlight Chaser (charge into a downward slam). Local Legend fires 5 waves of 5 fan-spread projectiles and self-damages when its slam is fully shield-blocked.
- **Tepetlisaur Whelp (Geo)** — Knee-high armored digger; oversized snout-drill, rocky back-plates, stubby legs.
  - behavior: Fodder. Headbutt · Burrow Strike (submerges, tracks you underground, erupts and slams).
- **Yumkasaur Whelp (Dendro)** — Knee-high fluffy-tailed lizardling with a long prehensile tongue; bouncy idle.
  - behavior: Fodder. Kick (bounces off its tail into a claw) · Grappling Strike (tongue-swings itself forward into a headbutt).
- **Koholasaur Whelp (Hydro)** — Small finned whale-lizard, sleek and glossy; awkward waddle on land.
  - behavior: Fodder, pure ranged. Aquashot — a single water projectile spat from the mouth, usable on land or in water.
- **Qucusaurus Chick (Pyro)** — Small feathered raptor-chick, orange plumage, hooked beak, oversized claws.
  - behavior: Fodder. Claw Strike (2 quick swipes) · Fireball (spat projectile).
- **Iktomisaurus Chick (Cryo)** — Small frost-crested glider with stubby membranous wings.
  - behavior: Fodder, ranged marker. Chilling Blast — marks a circle at your feet that detonates after a delay; sometimes fizzles and self-stuns the chick.
- **Tatankasaur Whelp (Electro)** — Knee-high shaggy bison-lizard, blunt horned head, low center of gravity.
  - behavior: Fodder. Melee headbutt · Charge (straight-line body slam).
- **Tepetlisaurus (Geo, adult)** — Low armored quadruped the size of a large boar — badger/armadillo proportions, huge drill-horn snout, layered rock plating along the spine.
  - behavior: Melee + burrow state. Melee Combo (drill-horn strike optionally into a jumping smash) · Burrow Strike — submerges, travels to you underground (sometimes surfacing to taunt), then erupts and slams for the big hit.
- **Yumkasaurus (Dendro, adult)** — Agile lizard with an enormous fluffy tail and a very long prehensile tongue; monkey-frog energy, springy posture.
  - behavior: Melee, mobile. Tail Whirl (spinning tail sweep) · Claw Strike (bounces off its tail into claws) · Grappling Somersault — tongue-swings itself forward into a flipping claw attack.
- **Koholasaurus (Hydro, adult)** — Torpedo-bodied whale-lizard, finned, broad tail, glossy blue hide. Sluggish on land, fast in water.
  - behavior: Ranged + charge. Tail Whirl (land only) · Aquashot (mouth-fired Hydro projectile, land or water) · Torrential Charge (water only).
- **Qucusaurus (Pyro, adult)** — Flying raptor-drake, big wings, hooked beak, blazing orange plumage, heavy talons.
  - behavior: Aerial melee + ranged. Claw Strike (2 swipes) · Fireball (spat projectile) · Diving Charge — dives from altitude and charges through the player.
- **Iktomisaurus (Cryo, adult)** — Wide-winged glider with a frost crest, membranous wings, pale-blue underside; rides its own wind currents.
  - behavior: Aerial ranged. Updraft (creates a wind current and goes airborne — still attacks while flying, and the player can use the current too) · Frosty Wind (forward projectile) · Chilling Blast (delayed marked circle). Local Legend adds Obsidian Storm: it gains a Frozen shield mid-air and, if unbroken in time, unleashes an arena-wide windstorm ending in a 300% blast — breaking the shield stuns it and drops it for bonus damage.
- **Tatankasaurus (Electro, adult)** — Big shaggy bison-saurian, low-slung head, heavy horns, thick hide. Reads placid until provoked.
  - behavior: Simple heavy melee. Stomp (forelegs, Electro AoE) · Charge (long straight-line body slam, stoppable by terrain).
- **Rock-Cavernous Wayob Manifestation (Geo, elite)** — Legless floating obsidian totem ~3–4 m tall: a stacked column of carved tribal faces ringed by a slowly rotating halo of glyph plates, Geo-gold light burning inside the carvings.
  - behavior: Shield state + zoned AoE. Crag Strike (jagged rocks erupt in an X, then a rectangle at your position) · Rotor Spin (3-tick spinning physical melee) · Shattering Box (delayed marked rectangle) · Arena: Moment of Trial — deploys a dome, gains a shield worth 111.4% of its max HP, is immune to all damage while warded, drains all party Energy inside, and fires X-pattern waves; failing to break it converts the ward to a Geo elemental shield, breaking it dumps huge energy particles and paralyzes it 5s.
- **Flow-Inverted Wayob Manifestation (Hydro, elite)** — Same obsidian totem column, Hydro-teal glow, water beading and running off the glyph plates.
  - behavior: Shield state + pull. Vacuum Burst (summons a vortex that drags you in, then a bubble detonates inside it) · Mini Bubble (delayed blast at your feet) · Rupturing Blast (charged water beam) · Arena: Moment of Trial (identical ward/Energy-drain/immunity cycle). Local Legend adds rolling Hydroflow Constructs and freezable water spouts.
- **Foliar-Swift Wayob Manifestation (Dendro, elite)** — Obsidian totem with green bioluminescence in the carvings and vine-growth on the halo plates.
  - behavior: Shield state + zoned AoE. Same elite template: Rotor Spin melee, delayed-marker Dendro zone attacks, and the Arena: Moment of Trial ward/Energy-drain/immunity cycle.
- **Biting-Cold Wayob Manifestation (Cryo, elite)** — Obsidian totem rimed with frost, pale-blue glow, ice shards orbiting the halo.
  - behavior: Shield state + zoned AoE, Cryo-coded; same Rotor Spin + delayed-marker + Arena: Moment of Trial template.
- **Burning-Aflame Wayob Manifestation (Pyro, elite)** — Obsidian totem cracked with magma veins, ember plume rising from the crown.
  - behavior: Shield state + zoned AoE, Pyro-coded; same Rotor Spin + delayed-marker + Arena: Moment of Trial template.
- **Thundering Wayob Manifestation (Electro, elite)** — Obsidian totem arcing with violet lightning between the halo plates.
  - behavior: Shield state + zoned AoE, Electro-coded; same Rotor Spin + delayed-marker + Arena: Moment of Trial template.
- **Kongamato (Dendro, Natlan Saurian normal boss)** — Enormous toad-lizard with a bulbous belly, spiked dorsal ridge and a very long tongue it uses as a grapple. Squats low, then rolls.
  - behavior: Big-body boss. Spit (ranged projectile) · Stomp → Tail Smash · Hanging Drop (tongue-swings above you, hangs, then slams) · Swinging Slam (tongue-swings in, slam + 2 expanding shockwaves) · Belly Spin (spins along the ground in a line, repeated ticks) · Flamegranate Spit — summons Flamegranates, devours them with its tongue, then fires ignited ones for Pyro plus lingering burning ground; the triple version ends in a 250% beam. Hitting the Flamegranates with Pyro first turns them into self-damage and can paralyze it.
- **Holawaqa Ngoubou (Pyro, Natlan Saurian normal boss)** — Colossal horned condor-bull hybrid with bladed wings used as forelimbs; transforms into a golden-flame form wreathed in fire.
  - behavior: Ground phase into an untouchable flight phase. Shockwave (both wings slammed) · Wing Swipe → Shockwave · Charge (into two wing slashes) · Sweep and Charge · Golden Flame Transformation — gains a Pyro shield and total damage immunity for 45s and enters a HIGH-ALTITUDE state melee cannot reach, alternating Flamewind Feather (two feather barrages plus a large projectile that plants a destructible feather) and Dive (100% AoE and stays high if a feather survives; 65% and drops to low-altitude if all feathers are destroyed).
- **Wayward Hermetic Spiritspeaker / "Sequestered Priestess" (Cryo, Sauroform Tribal Warrior normal boss)** — Human Mictlan priestess in flowing dark robes and a spirit mask, wielding an oversized paintbrush-staff; glides and vanishes. Tezcatepetonco Range.
  - behavior: Summoner boss with an untargetable phase. Paint Splash (staff spin + forward blast) · Double Sweep (2 brush strokes) · Gliding Sweep (blast-launch, glide in, 2 strokes) · Snowball (ranged) · Chilling Blast (3 delayed marked circles) · Radiant Reflections — returns to arena center, becomes INVISIBLE, and spawns 4 Cryo mirror-copies with 12.5% higher ATK that attack in relay combos; destroying the combo's last reflection briefly exposes and stuns her, destroying all four drops her out of the ritual paralyzed with -40% RES.

**Family structure:** Four-rung ladder, and it maps cleanly onto a StS-style fodder / elite / boss spine.

RUNG 0 — SWARM FODDER: Saurian whelps and chicks (Tepetlisaur/Yumkasaur/Koholasaur Whelp, Qucusaurus/Iktomisaurus Chick, Tatankasaur Whelp). Knee-high, 1–2 attacks each, one of which is literally "charge / spit / headbutt". Built for count-3-to-5 encounters.

RUNG 1 — LINE FODDER: the 18 Tribal Guardians (3 per tribe). Wiki text: "relatively low HP and ATK, primarily deal Physical DMG… all capable of a special attack matching their tribe's element." Three interchangeable weapon loadouts repeat across all six tribes — (a) sword/short-blade skirmisher, (b) claymore/heavy-hafted bruiser, (c) unarmed brawler with 2 thrown javelins/harpoons then knives. Re-dressed per tribe. This is the family's true "N-node normal" rung.

RUNG 2 — HEAVY NORMALS: wild adult Saurians (Tepetlisaurus, Yumkasaurus, Koholasaurus, Qucusaurus, Iktomisaurus, Tatankasaurus). Solo, large, 2–3 moves, one signature traversal-flavored attack (burrow, tongue-grapple, torrential charge, dive, updraft, charge). Sit above guardians, below crowned warriors.

RUNG 3 — CHAMPIONS / ELITES: the 12 Saurian-Crowned Warriors (2 per tribe). Wiki: "higher HP and ATK, capable of dealing both Physical and Elemental DMG." Bigger silhouettes wearing full saurian skull-crowns, wielding tribal-tech weapons: war hammer, powered saw, wide-mouth cannon, mounted slingshot. Each has a signature named finisher. Named overworld "Local Legend" copies add a whole extra layer (shields at HP thresholds, parry challenges, arena hazards, summoned destructibles) — that's the elite-plus tier. Alongside them, the 6 Wayob Manifestations (Flow-Inverted, Foliar-Swift, Rock-Cavernous, Biting-Cold, Burning-Aflame, Thundering) are the pure-elite shape: floating, shielded, arena-deploying, damage-immune-while-warded.

RUNG 4 — LEADERS / BOSSES: three "Normal Bosses" — Kongamato (Dendro saurian, tongue-swings + explosive Flamegranate ritual), Holawaqa Ngoubou (Pyro saurian, transforms into an untouchable high-altitude Golden Flame form), and Wayward Hermetic Spiritspeaker (Cryo human, Mictlan priestess who vanishes and fights through four destructible Radiant Reflections).

Note the family has NO true minion-summoner below boss tier. Every summon-shaped body (Spiritspeaker's Reflections, Kongamato's Flamegranates, Iktomisaurus Cryocrystal Cannon's barrels, Yumkasaurus Flowing Skyfire's grapple points) lives at rung 3–4. That is the family's one structural gap for reskin purposes.

### Raw pairing candidates (pre-curation)
- `inklets — 3× inklet, 11–17 HP, single {attack 4} (easy pool)` ↔ **Yumkasaur Whelp ×3** (strong) — Cited: tier05/content/act1_pool.yaml, easy pool `inklets`. Three tiny bodies each with exactly one chip attack. Yumkasaur Whelps are knee-high, bouncy, and canonically carry one or two moves — the Grappling Strike is already a single lunging hit. Silhouette, count, and HP-scale all land without touching a number; the swarm read (many small silhouettes, low individual threat) is exactly how whelp packs present in Natlan.
- `Exoskeleton — 24–28 HP, Skitter 1x3 / Mandibles 8 / Enrage +2 Str, 9 Hard to Kill; encounters of 3–4 (Act 2 easy+hard pools)` ↔ **Tepetlisaur Whelp ×3–4** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md:57, 73, 75. A pack of small ARMORED fodder that is annoying to finish off. Tepetlisaur Whelps are rock-plated diggers — 'Hard to Kill' reads as carapace, Skitter 1x3 reads as their scrabbling burrow-and-pop, Mandibles 8 as the drill headbutt. Same count band, same 'chip through the shell' feel.
- `Scroll of Biting — 31–38 HP each, Chomp 14 / More Teeth +2 Str / Chew 5x2, 2 Paper Cuts; fights of 3–4 (Act 3 easy+hard)` ↔ **Koholasaur Whelp ×3–4** (plausible) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md:105, 124, 127. A crowd of small biting bodies with a Strength ramp. Koholasaur Whelps are the family's only pure-ranged fodder (Aquashot), so Chomp/Chew have to be re-read as spit-bites — that's the one soft edge. Everything else (count, HP band, self-ramp on a hungry little animal) survives untouched. The 2 Paper Cuts status pollution needs a new flavor name but no mechanical change.
- `swarmling — 5× 14 HP, {attack 2} (tier0 SWARM battery)` ↔ **Qucusaurus Chick ×5** (plausible) — Cited: tier0/content/encounters/battery.yaml, `swarm`. Five identical featherweight bodies with one 2-damage peck; Qucusaurus Chicks are the family's smallest aggressive silhouette and their Claw Strike is literally a pair of tiny swipes. CAVEAT the [USER] should weigh: that file is marked '*** FROZEN 2026-07-19 — do not retune ***' and is the calibration harness, not shipped content — an art/name swap changes no number, but reskinning a measurement instrument may be undesirable on principle.
- `nibbit — 42–46 HP, Butt 12 / Hesitant Slice 6 / +5 Block / Hiss +2 Str (easy pool)` ↔ **Forged Sand Interrogator (Geo tribal guardian)** (plausible) — Cited: tier05/content/act1_pool.yaml, easy pool `nibbit`. The Interrogator's kit is Stab (single committed), Swing (a hesitant 2-beat that leaves her covering), and the Headspin windup — a visible crouch-and-wind before a drill-charge that reads perfectly as a self-buff beat. Low-HP, low-ATK guardian tier matches the 44 HP easy-pool slot exactly per the wiki's own 'relatively low HP and ATK' framing.
- `slime_group — leaf_slime (25–35, atk 5 / Frail 1) + twig_slime (25–35, atk 6 / Weak 1) (easy pool)` ↔ **Swiftstep Seeker (Dendro) + Flowing Blade Skirmisher (Hydro)** (plausible) — Cited: tier05/content/act1_pool.yaml, easy pool `slime_group`. The Grapplers' thrown javelins-then-knives (two charges, then a weaker repeat forever) is a natural body for Repeater Blast's escalating-then-settling rhythm. Softest point: Cubex's Strength snowball is bigger than anything a guardian canonically ramps.
- `Fabricator — 150 HP, Fabricate (summon bots) / Fabricating Strike 18 + summon / Disintegrate 11; minions Zapbot/Stabbot/Noisebot 18–23 and Guardbot 16–20 (Guard: +15 Block to Fabricator) (Act 3 hard pool)` ↔ **Wayward Hermetic Spiritspeaker (Cryo Sauroform Tribal Warrior normal boss) + Radiant Reflections** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md:111–113, 126. The best summoner match in the whole family, and the only one at the right tier. She spawns FOUR destructible copies with distinct roles, becomes untargetable behind them, and is exposed and stunned when the right one dies — that is Fabricate plus the Guardbot protection loop expressed as one animation set. Her Double Sweep and Paint Splash cover Fabricating Strike and Disintegrate. Tier caveat for the [USER]: she is a Normal Boss and Fabricator is an Act-3 normal, so this spends a leader-shaped body on a non-elite slot — worth it only if no other encounter needs her.
- `Frog Knight — 191 HP, Tongue Lash 13 + Frail / Strike Down Evil 21 / For the Queen +5 Str / Beetle Charge 35; Plating 15 (Act 3 hard pool)` ↔ **Tatankasaurus Warrior: Skybreaker (Electro Saurian-Crowned)** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md:114, 126. A big armored knight whose finisher is a charge. Skybreaker is the heaviest crowned silhouette — bison-horn crown, shaggy mantle, enormous two-handed hammer — and his signature literally 'charges towards the player and swings its hammer twice,' which is Beetle Charge 35 at full weight. Triple Smash (three escalating Electro impacts) covers Strike Down Evil, and For the Queen (+5 Str) reads as a tribal war-bellow. Plating 15 sits on visible plate armor. Only Tongue Lash needs re-flavoring into a hammer-haft jab.
- `Globe Head — 148 HP, Shocking Slap 13 + Frail / Thunder Strike 6x3 / Galvanic Burst 16 + Str; Galvanic 6 (Act 3 hard pool)` ↔ **Tatankasaurus Warrior: Spiritlight Chaser (Electro Saurian-Crowned)** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md:115, 126. Electro identity is already correct, which is rare — no elemental translation needed at all. Thunder Strike 6x3 maps onto Rushing Strike's dash-into-multi-hit and, in her Local Legend form, onto 5-wave fan volleys of Electro projectiles. Galvanic Burst 16 + Strength is Spiritlight Chaser, her charging downward slam. Shocking Slap + Frail lands as her slingshot pommel-strike. Fast in-and-out silhouette suits an enemy whose whole gimmick is escalating shock.
- `Owl Magistrate — 234 HP, Magistrate Scrutiny 16 / Peck Assault 4x6 / Judicial Flight (Soar) / Verdict 33 + Vulnerable (Act 3 hard pool)` ↔ **Holawaqa Ngoubou (Pyro Natlan Saurian normal boss)** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md:116, 126. Arguably the sharpest pairing in this atlas. Judicial Flight is a Soar state — Ngoubou's Golden Flame Transformation puts it in an explicit HIGH-ALTITUDE state that melee cannot reach. Peck Assault 4x6 is Flamewind Feather, which fires repeated feather barrages while airborne. Verdict 33 + Vulnerable is the Dive, a single huge AoE that ends the flight cycle. A colossal horned bird-beast passing sentence on you needs no reinterpretation whatsoever; the whole soar-then-descend intent shape is already the boss's published loop.
- `The Lost (93 HP, Debilitating Smog steals your Strength / Eye Lasers 4x2; Possess Strength) + The Forgotten (106 HP, Miasma steals your Dexterity and gains Block/Dex / Dread 13 + Dex bonus; Possess Speed) (Act 3 hard pool)` ↔ **Spiritspeaker Warrior: Determinator + Spiritspeaker Warrior: Psychokinetic (Cryo, Masters of the Night-Wind)** (strong) — Cited: docs/current/research/act2-act3-roster-research.md:118–119, 128. Stat-THEFT wants bodies whose lore is spirit-taking, and Mictlan — the Masters of the Night-Wind — are Natlan's death-and-spirit tribe: masked mediums with glowing eye-slits and trailing wind-ribbons. Their shared Infusion move is a visible cast that transfers power into the weapon, which is exactly the animation Debilitating Smog and Miasma need. Two same-tribe bodies with different weapon weights (sword vs sword, one lighter/faster) preserves the paired-enemy read. 'Determinator' and 'Psychokinetic' are already Strength- and mind/speed-coded names — a happy accident that lines up with Possess Strength and Possess Speed.
